again. HEAD is written the same way every other file is, by writing
`HEAD.lock` and renaming it over HEAD, so it loses the same race everything
else does. The branch ref (`refs/heads/catalog-gate`) was fine the whole time
and the commits were never at risk; what moved was which branch the working
tree and the next commit belonged to. **Two edits landed on `master`.**

So: `cat .git/HEAD` is the check, not `rev-parse` — and do it before every
commit, not once at the start. And never push from a session that has been
switching branches without reading that file first.

**A branch switch half-applies, silently, with exit code 0.** `git checkout
catalog-gate` restored the files that did not exist on master (it can CREATE)
and left every file that existed on both at master's content (it cannot
REPLACE). No error, no warning. The tree then typechecks as a mixture — here,
28 errors against a baseline of 10, all of them "X has no exported member Y"
between files that agree perfectly in the commit.

**Recovery, and it is simpler than the tar route above.** Writing to a file
works; only replacing it by rename does not. So:

```bash
for f in $(git --no-optional-locks status --porcelain | grep "^ M" | awk '{print $2}'); do
  git --no-optional-locks show "HEAD:$f" > "$f"
done
```

Run it after EVERY branch switch on this mount, then re-check `git status` —
it should print nothing but the files you meant to keep. Typecheck afterwards
and compare to the baseline; a jump of exactly the wrong kind (missing exports
between two files you just wrote together) is this trap, not your code.

The typecheck count and the test count after each round, moved here from the Commands table in `CLAUDE.md`. Compare COUNTS, never expect zero.

- **The Commands table's Tests row in `CLAUDE.md`, as it stood until Oct 9 2026** (moved here word for word when the first-session round's final measurement replaced it): **13,132** passing in 802 files on `claude/first-session-routine-plan-ui-2f8dc1` (Oct 9 2026: the first-session screens, Rounds 1 and 2, the whole-branch review's fixes and the screens preview's; typecheck 2; the rules unchanged by the review, rules tests as the round left them); **12,415** in 773 on `oct7/first-session` (Oct 7 2026; rules tests **328**); **12,385** in 771 on `oct7/ahead`, the last round on master (Oct 7 2026; typecheck 2; rules tests **323**; production build clean, 462.9 KB gzip on the first screen; `TZ=America/New_York npx vitest run --dir src --testTimeout=30000` in a worktree on AJ's PC, its files in LF). Run it as `TZ=America/New_York npx vitest run src` - see the date trap. On a loaded PC a few file-walking tests can pass vitest's 5 s limit; they pass alone or with `--testTimeout=30000`. A round records its own count in its round document and replaces the figure here; every earlier count, including this row as it stood until Oct 7 2026, is in `docs/KNOWN-TRAPS.md#baselines`

- **The Commands table's Tests row in `CLAUDE.md`, as it stood until Oct 7 2026** (moved here word for word when `CLAUDE.md` was trimmed: every session reads that file in full, and this row had grown to 24,456 characters; newest first): **12,385** passing in 771 files on `oct7/ahead` (Oct 7 2026: Ahead beside Month and the nightly record's dates counted from the day Mindbody counted, AJ's "1b 2a 3a", then the review's eight fixes, on master's `52108b56`; - **Budgets held by tests (they only go down):** the neutral ramp's `BARE_PALETTE_BUDGET` (`src/neutral-ramp.test.ts`) is **127** since the colour follow-ups (Oct 4 2026, the count: a trainer's profile edit drew its icons and home chip in Tailwind indigo and orange), **137** since the Navy Frame (Oct 4 2026, the count: the frame, the session sheets, the Critical strip and the sky and teal leftovers moved onto tokens), **219** since the calm Hub (Sep 28 2026; the Hub's card, grid and top moved onto tokens), 231 since the voice review follow-up (Sep 27 2026; 235 before — the Wrap-up left `ALWAYS_DARK_SCREENS` and the budget was cut to the real count). The codex's `HOSTED_OFF_SCALE_BUDGET` (`client-codex/kit/scale.test.ts`) is **26** (57 before: `relay/notes/notes.css` moved fully onto the type scale). My Studio's off-scale font-size budget (`my-studio/look.test.ts`) is **3**, all three in the Catalog's upkeep card (`.stu`). A test that fails on one of these is fixed in the stylesheet, never by raising the number.
- **The type scale is 11 · 12 · 14 · 17 · 22 · 30 since type and depth, phase 2 (Oct 4 2026). A scale change, not an exception.** 22 joined on purpose (a section title, a name in a sheet, a headline figure): `--cx-fs-22` in `client-codex/codex.tokens.css`, and each of the six tests that pin the scale gained the one number (`client-codex/kit/scale.test.ts` `SCALE_PX`, which also expects the token; `my-studio/look.test.ts`; `wiki/learning-scale.test.ts`; `client-profile/profile-nav-css.test.ts`; `components/WrapUpScreen.render.test.tsx`; `relay/notes/ClientJotStrip.render.test.tsx`). No existing declaration moved, and **the budgets did not rise**: `HOSTED_OFF_SCALE_BUDGET` stays 26 and My Studio's stays 3 (none of the files they count sets 22). 11 stays legal for the rules that already use it; nothing new uses it. The same phase made the body's weight a token, `--fw-body` 450 in `:root` and 420 in `.dark`, read by `body` in `@layer base`, and set `tracking-widest` to 0.08em (Tailwind's is 0.1em); `src/fonts.test.ts` holds all three. **The body weight reaches only text that inherits it**: 463 CSS declarations set 700 and 601 set 800 of their own (counted at phase 2), and `font-normal` is still 400, so a row label goes quieter rule by rule in a later phase, never through this token.
- **The depth tokens since type and depth, phase 3 (Oct 4 2026): tests that moved on purpose, none loosened.** `equipment/equipment-tokens.test.ts` gained a NAMED pattern: a token matching `--eq-(elev-0…5|elev-card|shelf|press|glow-live|glow-go|solid-light|go-light|panel-highlight)` must be `var(--` the same name `)`, the only values in the file that may alias outside `--eq-*`; every other value still matches the colour pattern, and the fallback is still `.dark` key for key. `palette-copies.test.ts` added the depth keys to each copy's `mirrors` (the Pulse less its two orange ones) and the four `--psub-*` aliases to the profile shell's list; `studio-tasks/studio-tokens.test.ts` added each `--st-*` depth key to `FROM_THE_APP`; `core-tokens.test.ts` counts `--raised`, `--well` and `--tray` as surfaces that are never white in light and navy in dark, and measures words on a raised control, in a well and on the tray, and `--input` on `--raised` at 3:1. The dark `--elev-1` to `--elev-5` are stronger than before (they had "collapsed to almost nothing"); every `shadow-sm` to `shadow-2xl` reads them, so dialogs, toasts and menus lift a little more in both modes from this phase on.

- **beta-prep trim (Sep 17 2026), on the `beta-prep` branch only:** typecheck 11; 2,982 tests (2,977 on master, plus five new pins: the History button's `openProfileAt`, the two starting-weight casing tests, the two LoginScreen render tests).
